    WHEN EXISTS (
        SELECT 1
        FROM [Appointments] AS [a]
        WHERE [a].[DoctorId] = @doctorId AND CONVERT(date, [a].[ScheduledDate]) = @date_Date AND [a].[TimeSlot] = @timeSlot AND [a].[Status] <> N'Cancelled') THEN CAST(1 AS bit)
